**[00:05:11.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000099)  
We are here to deliberate on the proposed

**[00:05:13.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000100)  
budget of the Department of Transportation

**[00:05:16.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000101)  
for Fiscal Year 2027.

**[00:05:19.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000102)  
Araw-araw,

**[00:05:19.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000103)  
milyong-milyong Pilipino ang bumabiyahe papunta sa

**[00:05:21.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000104)  
trabaho, paaralan, ospital, at

**[00:05:23.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000105)  
pauwi sa kanilang pamilya.

**[00:05:25.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000106)  
For an ordinary Filipino,

**[00:05:27.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000107)  
transportation not simply about infrastructure,

**[00:05:29.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000108)  
usapin ito ng oras, gastos,

**[00:05:32.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000109)  
kaligtasan, at kalidad ng buhay.

**[00:05:34.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000110)  
For 2027,

**[00:05:36.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000111)  
the National Expenditure Program

**[00:05:37.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000112)  
proposes

**[00:05:38.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000113)  
302.89 billion

**[00:05:41.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000114)  
for the DOTR.

**[00:05:43.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000115)  
If fully approved, this will be the largest

**[00:05:45.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000116)  
DOTR budget over the

**[00:05:47.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000117)  
three-year period from 2025

**[00:05:49.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000118)  
to 2027.

**[00:05:52.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000119)  
A substantial portion of this budget

**[00:05:54.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000120)  
will support major transportation

**[00:05:55.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000121)  
infrastructure, particularly

**[00:05:57.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000122)  
our railway projects.

**[00:05:59.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000123)  
But our commuters will not measure success

**[00:06:01.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000124)  
by how much we appropriate.

**[00:06:04.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000125)  
Ang sukatan nila ay simple.

**[00:06:06.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000126)  
Mas mabilis ba ang biyahe?

**[00:06:08.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000127)

**[00:06:09.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000128)

**[00:06:12.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000129)

**[00:06:14.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000130)

**[00:06:16.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000131)

**[00:06:18.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000132)

**[00:06:20.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000133)

**[00:08:13.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000191)  
So, to continue, we must also look beyond

**[00:08:15.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000192)

**[00:08:17.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000193)

**[00:08:18.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000194)

**[00:08:20.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000195)

**[00:08:23.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000196)

**[00:08:25.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000197)

**[00:08:29.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000199)

**[00:08:33.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000200)  
the department's foreign-assisted projects.

**[00:08:36.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000201)

**[00:08:38.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000202)

**[00:08:41.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000203)

**[00:08:43.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000204)

**[00:08:46.020] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000205)

**[00:08:49.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000206)

**[00:08:51.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000207)

**[00:08:52.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000208)

**[00:08:54.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000209)

**[00:08:57.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000210)

**[00:08:59.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000211)

**[00:17:19.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-000523)

Ito po ay nagpapatunay lamang

**[00:27:58.980] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000856)

Next slide po.

**[00:28:33.380] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000872)

Ito po ay para sa

**[00:28:46.080] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000878)  
billion ang naka-allocate

po para sa foreign

**[00:28:49.140] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000880)

**[00:28:50.600] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000881)  
At 5 billion lang po

**[00:28:54.440] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000883)

po tayong allocation na

**[00:28:58.540] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000885)  
2.9 billion para

**[00:29:00.640] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000886)  
po sa mostly

**[00:29:02.700] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000887)  
for the ROW requirements

**[00:29:05.140] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000888)  
ng ating

**[00:29:05.860] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000889)  
mga different projects.

**[00:29:08.620] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000890)  
Ang equipment outlay po, those

**[00:29:10.660] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000891)  
are the regular equipment outlay

**[00:29:12.940] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000892)  
na kailangan po ng ating

**[00:29:14.580] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000893)  
mga opisina.

**[00:29:16.780] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000894)  
So, ang sumunod

**[00:29:18.880] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000895)  
po is yung establishment

**[00:29:20.440] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000896)  
of headquarters ng PCG

**[00:29:22.600] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000897)  
with an allocation of

**[00:29:24.220] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000898)  
1.7 billion.

**[00:29:29.600] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000899)  
Ito po ay comparison

**[00:29:31.720] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000900)  
ng amount na iprinopose po namin.

**[00:29:35.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000901)  
Assistant Secretary

**[00:29:36.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000902)  
Bien,

**[00:29:38.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000903)  
balik po tayo doon sa

**[00:29:40.120] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000904)  
previous frame.

**[00:29:42.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000905)  
Yun po sinasabing

**[00:29:44.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000906)  
yung 1.7 billion

**[00:29:46.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000907)  
sa Philippine Coast Guard. Kasama na po

**[00:29:48.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000908)  
ba dyan yung sinasabing headquarters?

**[00:29:50.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000909)  
Kasama, yan ho, PCG

**[00:29:52.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000910)  
establishment of HQ. Kasama

**[00:29:54.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000911)  
po ba dyan yung pagtatayo po?

**[00:29:56.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000912)  
Request nila ng buildings

**[00:29:58.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000913)  
o ng...

**[00:30:00.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000914)  
stations, sa iba't-ibang mga islets dyan po sa Pagasa Island. Is that included?

**[00:30:10.100] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000915)  
Hindi po, sir. Ito po ay para dun lang po sa tinatawag po nilang panibagong headquarters po nila.

**[00:30:19.080] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000916)  
Pwede po tayong mangingin ng detalya sa ating kasamang taga-PCG?

**[00:30:23.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000917)  
Thank you, Asec Bien. Go ahead po, Vice Admiral Ibañez. May request po kasi, I think during the visit of the Secretary and myself dun po sa Pagasa, yung Coast Guard po,

**[00:30:42.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000918)  
Commandant natin, was requesting na lagyan po ng mga stations, ang iba't-ibang small islands po around Pagasa o around Kalayaan.

**[00:30:50.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000919)  
Saan po? Sinama niyo po ba sa inyong budget for this year, yung mga substations and or stations ng Philippine Coast Guard?

**[00:31:02.880] PCG Vice Admiral Edgar Ybañez** (asr-000920)  
Good morning, Sir Chair. Good morning, Vice Chair and our Senate President. Sir, with regards to your query, Sir.

**[00:31:15.700] PCG Vice Admiral Edgar Ybañez** (asr-000921)  
Actually, Sir, ni-request namin, Sir, but it was not approved in the NEP, Sir.

**[00:31:20.620] PCG Vice Admiral Edgar Ybañez** (asr-000922)  
Hindi po na-approve yun.

**[00:31:22.650] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000922-split-2)  
Would you happen to know quickly lamang po para makabalik tayo kay Asec kung ilang pong mga stations po ni-request?

**[00:31:27.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000923)  
Kasi as far as I can recall, I was, sinabi po sa akin parang two or three stations po. Maybe Secretary Giovanni can answer it.

**[00:31:38.080] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000924)  
I confirm, Mr. Vice Chairperson. Tama po kayo. Ang PCG po, nag-submit po sila ng mga iba't-ibang mga pondo para sa mga istasyon po natin. Mga tatlo nga po yun.

**[00:31:50.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000925)  
Marami pa nga po doon actually po. Mas lalong nung tayo nagkita po sa West Philippine Sea. Ito po yung mga proposal. Pero unfortunately po, hindi po nasama sa ating NEP.

**[00:32:01.580] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000926)  
Ito po yung pinanggagalingan ko po kanina, Mr. Chair, na may mga iba't-ibang proyekto rin po tayo, programa na talagang kailangan. But unfortunately, hindi nasama sa NEP. Kaya po kami makikiusap po sa inyo.

**[00:32:14.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000927)  
Thank you po, Secretary. Go ahead po, Assistant Secretary Bien.

**[00:32:20.620] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000928)  
Salamat sa aking pong pagpapatuloy. Nais ko lamang pong i-present sa inyo na in terms of total number of projects, nag-propose po kami ng total of 179 projects. Subalit ang naibigay lang po sa amin sa NEP ay tatlumpu po. So out of the 179 projects we proposed, we were only given allocation for 30 projects.

**[00:32:46.740] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000929)  
In terms of amount, our total proposal.

**[00:32:50.620] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000930)  
For projects was 624 billion, but we were only given 237 billion. So dito po, sa susunod pong table, makikita po natin supposedly kung paano po namin i-prenopose yung aming mga projects.

**[00:33:09.120] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000931)  
We proposed for 22 projects for rail. However, we were only given 6 projects. Allocation for 6 projects under the NEP.

**[00:33:18.720] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000932)  
We also proposed 9.

**[00:33:20.620] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000933)  
19 projects for the road transport, totaling to 37.2 billion. However, we were only given 7 projects. Allocation for 7 projects, totaling to 15 billion.

**[00:33:35.320] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000934)  
Aviation, we proposed 42 projects, totaling to 23 billion, but we were only given allocation for 10 projects, amounting to 5.8 billion.

**[00:33:48.100] DOTr Assistant Secretary Cecilia E. Bien** (asr-000935)  
So maritime naman po ang total.

**[00:33:50.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000936)  
I'm sorry, Asec. I'm sorry with the indulgence of our chair.

**[00:33:57.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000937)  
Ma'am, may problema po tayo. Ito po yung isang problema. You've been talking about aviation. Kasama po ba dyan yung mga airports po natin? Is that what we're talking about?

**[00:34:08.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000938)  
Yes, sir. Improvement po of our airports nationwide.

**[00:34:12.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000939)  
We have a problem. We're facing a big problem. Yung mga tourist destinations po natin, bumaba lahat because of the fact.

**[00:34:20.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000940)  
So ng mga turboprop na airplanes natin, they cannot land and take off sa Naia. So ang option po is sa Clark.

**[00:34:31.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000941)  
Kung ako po ang turista, tiga Manila, hindi po ako pupunta ng Clark para sumakay, para pupunta ng Boracay or pupunta ng Busuanga. Marami po tayo mga islands within na ikang turboprop ang ginagamit.

**[00:34:44.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000942)  
Pero because of the travel time po eh, from Manila to. My question now is, well, di ba po?

**[00:34:50.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000943)  
Di ba may pinagpag-uusap before na we're going to do something, we're going to develop it pong Sangli?

**[00:34:57.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000944)  
Well, whatever happened to Sangli po, parang wala na nangyayari ho dyan. Can we develop Sangli para doon na lang mag-take off landing yung mga turboprop?

**[00:35:06.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000945)  
Madali na lang po, mas malapit-lapit. Siguro, barge na lang po galing po sa Cavite, papunta po, tatawid na lang po yun. Mas malapit.

**[00:35:13.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000946)  
Kaysa sa pupunta pa ng Clark, takes about one hour to two hours ang biyahe po going to Clark.

**[00:35:20.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000947)  
Ano dyan sa Sangli?

**[00:35:23.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000948)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair, Your Honor. May dalawa pong aspeto itong Sangli natin.

**[00:35:27.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000949)  
Yung Sangli na airport na sinasabi natin, domestic airport, yan po ay nasa DOTR at sa Kakaab.

**[00:35:34.340] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000950)  
Pero yung sinasabi po natin na mas malaking Sangli International Airport, yan po ay isang JV with the Provincial Government of Cavite.

**[00:35:41.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000951)  
Well, hindi ho sa National Government.

**[00:35:45.560] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000952)  
Ganun pa man, ang DOTR at ang Kakaab ay nakipagtulungan po.

**[00:35:50.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000953)  
Doon sa probinsya ng Cavite, kasama na po yung mga proponent ng ating Sangli International Airport, pagdating sa mga technical po na gawain sa aviation, tayo po sa DOTR at Kakaab.

**[00:36:04.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000954)  
Pero ito po ay isang malaking proyekto po. Ang kasama din po dito ay pagkikipag-ugnayan, pagkikipag-usap sa Philippine Navy po natin.

**[00:36:12.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000955)  
Yung Sangli po natin ay hindi lang po DOTR itself, nandyan pa po yung Philippine Navy natin.

**[00:36:18.660] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000956)  
If I may say, Mr. Chairman,

**[00:36:20.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000957)  
Just last, over the weekend po, isa po yan sa mga pinag-usapan po ng ating Pangulo also, doon po sa state visit po natin sa India, kasama po ang GMR, kasama din po yung proponent po ng ating Sangli International Airport.

**[00:36:36.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000958)  
So madaling sabi po, Mr. Chairman, tuloy naman po ang proyekto, kasama ang Provincial Government of Cavite, at ang DOTR at Kakaab ay nakipagtulungan po sa kanila.

**[00:36:45.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000959)  
Kasi po, Secretary, Mr. Chair, the problem po,

**[00:36:50.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000960)  
bumaba po talaga lahat ng tourist destinations.

**[00:36:53.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000961)  
I went around the country po these past few months.

**[00:36:56.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000962)  
Lahat po nagreklamo. There was a drop in their ikanga tourist arrivals.

**[00:37:01.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000963)  
And when you ask naman po mga travel agencies, ang sagot po sa atin, kasi sir, pupunta pa sila ng Clark eh, bibiyahe pa.

**[00:37:08.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000964)  
Hindi kagaya dito, mga tiga Quezon City, Pasay, Barikina, etc.

**[00:37:13.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000965)  
Tiga NCR,

**[00:37:15.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000966)  
nandiretso na na po sa naiya, pero siyempre nag-e-expand po tayo.

**[00:37:18.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000967)  
At ang ginawa naman po ng,

**[00:37:20.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000968)  
company po ni nang naiya ngayon,

**[00:37:22.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000969)  
nung privatized po yan,

**[00:37:24.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000970)  
magpapatakbo yan,

**[00:37:26.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000971)  
para ma-de-congest.

**[00:37:27.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000972)  
Maganda naman po, I mean, yung intention.

**[00:37:29.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000973)  
Alisin po yung mga turboprop.

**[00:37:31.100] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000974)  
Pero nga lang po,

**[00:37:32.440] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000975)  
nag-suffer naman po yung tourist arrivals natin

**[00:37:35.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000976)  
sa iba't-ibang mga major.

**[00:37:37.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000977)  
Nakakaya po ako, I'm from Palawan,

**[00:37:39.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000978)  
bumaba po yung ikanga arrivals po sa El Nido,

**[00:37:42.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000979)  
sa Coron, sa Busuanga,

**[00:37:44.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000980)  
dahil nga po, tapos may mga burakay din.

**[00:37:46.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000981)  
Or even dito po sa Batanes,

**[00:37:49.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000982)  
bumaba po.

**[00:37:50.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000983)  
Layo pa po, bibiyahin eh.

**[00:37:52.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000984)  
Can we do something na,

**[00:37:54.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000985)  
ikanga mapabilis, mapapastruck,

**[00:37:56.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000986)  
na ilipat na lang natin ang turboprop

**[00:37:58.220] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000987)  
ng mga airplanes,

**[00:37:59.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000988)  
diyan po mag-landing and take-off

**[00:38:01.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-000989)  
sa ikanga airport na yan?

**[00:38:05.880] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000990)  
Yes, Mr. Chairman, Your Honor.

**[00:38:07.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000991)  
Kaya nga po, ang pulisiya po natin ngayon sa DOTR,

**[00:38:11.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000992)  
ay itong pagpapahaba ng runway po.

**[00:38:14.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000993)  
Kailangan natin gawin yan,

**[00:38:15.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000994)  
yung pagpapahaba ng runway.

**[00:38:16.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000995)  
Itong mga turboprop po kasi,

**[00:38:18.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000996)  
na sinasabi natin,

**[00:38:20.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000997)  
iyan lang po yung pipwede sa mga malilit na runway.

**[00:38:23.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000998)  
And usually, ito yung mga tourist spot natin.

**[00:38:26.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-000999)  
At bagamat tumingi kami ng pondo,

**[00:38:29.340] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001000)  
para kahit papaano,

**[00:38:30.660] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001001)  
yun yung unahin natin,

**[00:38:32.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001002)  
pagpapahaba at pagpapalapad ng ating mga runway,

**[00:38:35.900] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001003)  
hindi po tayo nabigyan ng sapat na pondo sa NEP natin.

**[00:38:39.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001004)  
So, nakikiusap tayo sa KAAP,

**[00:38:41.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001005)  
na pondohan na po nila yung mga runway.

**[00:38:44.180] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001006)  
We identified two, Mr. Chairman, if I may say.

**[00:38:47.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001007)  
Itong sa ating Busuanga po,

**[00:38:50.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001008)  
dire-diretso na po yung pagpapalapad at pagpapahaba po ng ating runway dyan.

**[00:38:54.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001009)  
At hopefully, we can finish it by second quarter of next year.

**[00:38:58.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001010)  
Upang sa ganon,

**[00:38:59.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001011)  
yung mga babiyahe pong turista,

**[00:39:01.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001012)  
makagamit na nila yung bigger plane, yung mga jet engine plane natin,

**[00:39:05.360] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001013)  
at dito na po sa Manila yan.

**[00:39:07.220] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001014)  
At hindi na kailangan sa Clark.

**[00:39:09.220] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001015)  
So, yun pa rin po ang magiging kailangan maging rason.

**[00:39:12.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001016)  
At yun dapat ang tamang pulisiya,

**[00:39:14.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001017)  
na pahabaan yung mga runway.

**[00:39:16.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001018)  
At hindi lang po na makakabawas kasi po yan,

**[00:39:19.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001019)  
sa pamasahe din po ng ating mga turista at sa mga mananakay.

**[00:39:23.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001020)  
Mas mabilis po ba ang gawin yung pagpapahaba ng runway?

**[00:39:27.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001021)  
O paglilipat nila ng takeoff at gagawing airport ng domestic flights

**[00:39:31.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001022)  
ng mga DLAC na aeroplano,

**[00:39:33.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001023)  
ang paglilipat po dyan sa Sangli rather than pagpapahaba?

**[00:39:37.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001024)  
Ano po mas mabilis na ikaw nga maisakatuparan?

**[00:43:03.020] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-001123)  
Kaya pinagtutuunan natin na mabuti ang ating mga tren, mga busway, transport hub at integrated terminal

**[00:43:11.080] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-001124)  
at ang pagpapaganda at pagpapalawak ng mga paliparan at pantalan sa buong bansa.

**[00:43:16.360] President Bongbong Marcos (video)** (asr-001125)  
Ito ay upang mas malawak at malayo ang naaabot, tumaas ang kapasidad at maging moderno at mas maginhawa ang serbisyo.

**[00:43:29.800] Presentation video** (asr-001125-split-2)  
The Department of Transportation has been relentless in achieving significant strides to improve the country's public transport system. President Ferdinand Marcos Jr. has ordered the DOTr to expedite the construction of critical big-ticket transport infrastructure projects and initiate commuter-focused

**[00:43:46.360] Presentation video** (asr-001126)  
programs to ease the daily commute of Filipinos. Among the commuter-centric initiatives the DOTr has implemented were the 50% discounts for senior citizens, students and persons with disabilities, the One P3 Familia Pass promo and 12 days of Christmas Libreng Sakay across major lines to encourage commuters and motorists to use public transport and at the same time help them save on transport costs. The government also relaunched the iconic Love Bus

**[00:44:16.360] Presentation video** (asr-001127)  
Libreng Sakay initiative through the service contracting program, providing free and reliable public transportation in major urban areas including Metro Manila, Metro Cebu and Metro Davao. Personalized white beep cards with an automatic 50% discount were distributed to senior citizens, PWDs and students riding MRT-3, LRT-1 and LRT-2. The DOTr continues to improve MRT-3 facilities, ensuring passenger

**[00:44:46.360] Presentation video** (asr-001128)  
comfort and safety while eliminating long queues and speeding up commuter entry at stations. Tension in the Middle East early this year made a heavy impact in many countries, including the Philippines. Under Uplift, the DOTr and its attached agencies

**[00:45:00.000] Presentation video** (asr-001129)  
implemented recalibrated support packages that include fare discounts, free rides, fuel subsidy, terminal fee discount, toll discount, rebates on transport charges, and the service contracting program, among others.

**[00:45:14.360] Presentation video** (asr-001130)  
All this to cushion the impact of the Middle East crisis on commuters and motorists.

**[00:45:18.580] Presentation video** (asr-001131)  
As part of the Uplift initiatives, President Marcos Jr. led the launch of the service contracting program at Araneta Center Bus Terminal in Quezon City and the fuel subsidy program at Parañaque Integrated Terminal Exchange.

**[00:45:32.320] Presentation video** (asr-001132)  
The DOTR likewise continues to implement a 50% fare discount to all passengers with MRT-3 and LRT-2.

**[00:45:41.220] Presentation video** (asr-001133)  
In the railway sector, the DOTr is speeding up the construction of mass transit railway systems.

**[00:45:47.360] Presentation video** (asr-001134)  
One of the biggest is

**[00:45:48.560] Presentation video** (asr-001135)  
the Metro Manila Subway Project, which is fast taking shape underground from Valenzuela to NAIA.

**[00:45:55.220] Presentation video** (asr-001136)  
Once operational, the 33-kilometer 17-station Metro Manila Subway Project, which will run from Valenzuela City to Naia, will reduce travel time from 90 minutes to just 41 minutes, and will benefit around 519,000 passengers daily.

**[00:46:11.680] Presentation video** (asr-001137)  
On the other hand, the North-South Commuter Railways Valenzuela to Malolo segment will be operational next year.

**[00:46:18.560] Presentation video** (asr-001138)  
Once fully operational, the 147-kilometer rail line can service 800,000 passengers daily, reducing travel time between Clark-Pampanga and Calamba-Laguna from over 4 hours to just 2 hours.

**[00:46:31.680] Presentation video** (asr-001139)  
Preparations for the NSCR's operations and maintenance are underway, through the public-private partnership market-sounding activities being held in different countries.

**[00:46:40.400] Presentation video** (asr-001140)  
Relocation activities are also ongoing and progressing in Cavite, Laguna, and Pampanga to support communities affected by the said project.

**[00:46:48.560] Presentation video** (asr-001141)  
Meanwhile, the MRT-7's first 12 stations are expected to be operational by second quarter of next year, connecting North Avenue in Quezon City to Sacred Heart in Caloocan.

**[00:46:59.720] Presentation video** (asr-001142)  
Once the project is completed, connecting Quezon City all the way to Bulacan will cut travel time from 2 to 3 hours to just 35 minutes, and can serve up to 850,000 passengers daily.

**[00:47:11.840] Presentation video** (asr-001143)  
Existing rail infrastructure is also being rehabilitated to provide an improved and more convenient travel experience for commuters.

**[00:47:18.560] Presentation video** (asr-001144)  
The rehabilitation of MRT-3's Quezon Avenue station offers clearer and larger wayfinding signs, cleaner and more accessible restrooms, wider passenger spaces, improved ventilation, and platform window louvers and tactile tiles for persons with visual impairment.

**[00:47:37.260] Presentation video** (asr-001145)  
These upgrades are designed to make commuting easier, and as Quezon Avenue station sets the pace for improvements, other MRT-3 stations are set to follow suit as rehabilitation works continue.

**[00:47:48.560] Presentation video** (asr-001146)  
At the center of Metro Manila's growing rail network is the Common Station, or the Unified Grand Central Station.

**[00:47:57.440] Presentation video** (asr-001147)  
By connecting LRT-1, MRT-3, and MRT-7, it will ensure seamless transfers for at least 1.28 million passengers.

**[00:48:07.060] Presentation video** (asr-001148)  
To the south of Manila, the LRT-1 Cavite Extension is bringing rail service closer to Paranaque, Espinas, and Cavite.

**[00:48:15.040] Presentation video** (asr-001149)  
The 11.7-kilometer extension with eight new

**[00:48:18.560] Presentation video** (asr-001150)  
stations, is expected to reduce

**[00:48:20.720] Presentation video** (asr-001151)  
Baclaran to Bacor travel time

**[00:48:22.540] Presentation video** (asr-001152)  
from around 70 minutes to only

**[00:48:24.500] Presentation video** (asr-001153)  
25 minutes. And beyond

**[00:48:26.720] Presentation video** (asr-001154)  
Metro Manila, the Mindanao

**[00:48:28.560] Presentation video** (asr-001155)  
Railway project is set to improve the

connectivity in Davao region.

Its first phase spans

**[00:59:03.320] Presentation video** (asr-001486)  
for the benefit of the Filipino people.

**[00:59:06.620] Presentation video** (asr-001487)  
The commitment was affirmed when,

**[00:59:08.520] Presentation video** (asr-001488)  
for the first time in over three decades,

**[00:59:10.780] Presentation video** (asr-001489)  
the DOTR received an unmodified opinion

**[00:59:13.260] Presentation video** (asr-001490)  
from the Commission on Audit

**[00:59:14.540] Presentation video** (asr-001491)  
for Fiscal Year 2025,

**[00:59:17.480] Presentation video** (asr-001493)  
the agency's compliance

**[00:59:18.620] Presentation video** (asr-001494)  
with applicable financial reporting standards

**[00:59:21.220] Presentation video** (asr-001495)  
and proper stewardship of public funds.

**[00:59:24.640] Presentation video** (asr-001496)  
These accomplishments

**[00:59:25.360] Presentation video** (asr-001497)  
in the transportation sector

**[00:59:26.900] Presentation video** (asr-001498)  
have opened up a broader spectrum

**[00:59:28.600] Presentation video** (asr-001499)  
of opportunities

**[00:59:29.440] Presentation video** (asr-001500)  
for the country's development,

**[00:59:31.380] Presentation video** (asr-001501)  
helping strengthen the economy

**[00:59:32.700] Presentation video** (asr-001502)  
and support long-term growth.

**[00:59:35.140] Presentation video** (asr-001503)  
By addressing bottlenecks and gaps

**[00:59:36.760] Presentation video** (asr-001504)  
in transportation infrastructure,

**[00:59:39.060] Presentation video** (asr-001505)  
the DOTR remains steadfast

**[00:59:40.820] Presentation video** (asr-001506)  
in its trust and long-term vision

**[00:59:42.380] Presentation video** (asr-001507)  
of improving mobility

**[00:59:43.880] Presentation video** (asr-001508)  
and strengthening connectivity

**[00:59:45.360] Presentation video** (asr-001509)  
across the archipelago.

**[00:59:46.900] Presentation video** (asr-001513)  
With several projects set for completion,

**[00:59:49.980] Presentation video** (asr-001514)  
the President assures Filipinos

**[00:59:51.640] Presentation video** (asr-001515)  
of its chief goal,

**[00:59:53.120] Presentation video** (asr-001516)  
to make their daily commute

**[00:59:55.020] Presentation video** (asr-001517)  
convenient and comfortable.

**[01:00:00.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001518)  
Mr. Chairman, Your Honors, thank you for your patience. We're ready to answer some questions.

**[01:00:05.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001519)  
Okay, thank you. Secretary Lopez, before the Chair will propound questions, I'll ask my colleagues first. Our Senate President?

**[01:00:17.840] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-001520)  
I'll defer to Senator Erwin.

**[01:00:20.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-001521)  
Senator Erwin, according to arrival, so Senator Tulfo, you may now ask questions.

**[01:00:28.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001522)  
All right, thank you, Mr. Chair. Secretary Lopez, sir, napansin ko po, pinag-uusapan nga namin ni Siguro Sapanho ito sa KAAP, but do we have plans to ikang lagyan ng mga tubes yung Puerto Princesa City Airport?

**[01:00:50.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001523)  
Taga doon po ako eh. I mean, naglagay po tayo ng airport and tinawag po nating international.

**[01:00:56.780] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001524)  
Pag umambon lang po, eh hindi na makababa ang pasahero o may payong kaagad. Pag umaraw, payong. I was wondering, sino ang nagdasinong kula po ang nagdesign ng airport na yun?

**[01:01:09.320] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001525)  
At mga turista, nagagalit nga po eh. Gusto po pumunta ng El Nido, gusto po pumunta doon sa mga beaches sa Puerto Princesa, wala hong tube. Hindi ko maintindihan. Bakit po ganun?

**[01:01:20.960] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001526)  
Thank you po, Mr. Chair. Your Honor, tama po kayo. At maalala ko pa po, tumawag po kayo kung bakit wala pong tube.

**[01:01:26.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001527)  
Yung ating Puerto Princesa. The Puerto Princesa Airport was ano po yan? Korea-funded project po yan. Keksim Bank po yan. But when you raise that concern po, Mr. Chairman, Your Honor, magiging pulisiya na po ng DOTR kasama ang KAAP na simula ngayon lahat ng ating malalaking airport at kung ito naman ay pwede maging international airport,

**[01:01:53.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001528)  
the design itself right from the start.

**[01:01:56.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001529)  
Merong provision for passenger boarding bridge.

**[01:02:00.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001530)  
Ngayon po ang ginagawa namin sa mga, sabi natin, mga malilit na airport natin, mga iba't ibang airport sa probinsya,

**[01:02:07.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001531)  
nagpapagawa kami nitong, kasi nga, papag mas talagang matatanda, mainit, mulan,

**[01:02:11.880] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001532)  
ang layo ho eh, ang nilalakad nila mula sa terminal, papunta kung saan yung aeroplano natin.

**[01:02:17.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001533)  
Isa po sa mga inisiyatibong pinapairal natin ngayon ay yung sinasabi nating,

**[01:02:26.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001534)  
Air Stairs, maybe I can defer to KAAP, pero gagawin po namin yan sa policy na po mula ngayon.

**[01:02:35.300] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001535)  
But, Sec. Lopez, before you refer to KAAP, Puerto Princesa is international airport, correct?

**[01:02:42.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001536)  
Tama po.

**[01:02:43.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001537)  
Pero the design po was through, keksimbang po yan, we just said that time.

**[01:02:49.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001538)  
Maybe what we can do right now, Mr. Chairman, I'm finding ways out.

**[01:02:53.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001539)  
Secretary, I was wondering, sila po yan.

**[01:02:56.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001540)  
Pero wala ba tayong pakialam sa design na common sense, we'll say like,

**[01:03:02.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001541)  
lagyan natin, may mga senior citizens, tama po kayo eh, malayo po eh, dito pa pa parada.

**[01:03:07.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001542)  
Binilang ko po parang, from pagbaba mo ng aeroplano hanggang doon sa arrival lounge ng Puerto Princesa eh,

**[01:03:15.980] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001543)  
parang nakaka 2,000 steps ka na eh.

**[01:03:18.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001544)  
Kunti nilang lalakarin mo, parang may 10,000 steps ka na.

**[01:03:21.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001545)  
So hindi ko alam bakit inaprubahan po yan.

**[01:03:24.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001546)  
Well, alam ko po, hindi pa hunin yung panahon.

**[01:03:26.360] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001547)  
Pero dapat siguro next time, tama po kayo, yung policy na natin ngayon,

**[01:03:31.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001548)  
na pag malalaking airport, dapat po eh.

**[01:03:34.280] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001549)  
So anong planong gawin dyan?

**[01:03:37.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001550)  
General Rosario, sir, what are we going to do?

**[01:03:40.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001551)  
Hindi na natin lalagyan ng tube yan? Ganun na lang yun?

**[01:03:44.960] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001552)  
Pinag-aaralan po natin kung pwede pa.

**[01:03:46.880] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001553)  
Pero magiging malaking trabaho po dahil may structural implications.

**[01:03:54.480] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001554)  
Sa ngayon, yung sinabi po ni Secretary,

**[01:03:56.360] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001555)  
ay nalagyan muna ng air stairs,

**[01:03:58.760] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001556)  
which is nakaprogram na po for procurement.

**[01:04:01.680] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001557)  
Dalawa ang naka-allocate for Puerto Princesa

**[01:04:04.620] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001558)  
at gayon din po sa ibang airport for convenience po ng mga pasayero.

**[01:04:10.720] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001559)  
So air stairs lang po lalagyan natin.

**[01:04:13.000] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001560)  
So paano pag umulan o wala rin?

**[01:04:14.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001561)  
Or umambon?

**[01:04:16.840] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001562)  
Yung design po ng air stairs natin ay may cover.

**[01:04:20.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001563)  
Ah, okay.

**[01:04:21.160] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001564)  
Pagka dating sa babae, ganun po.

**[01:04:23.200] CAAP Director General Raul L. Del Rosario** (asr-001565)  
Talagang papayungan ng mga airline.

**[01:04:26.880] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001566)  
Paano po yung worry ko lang po, mga senior citizens po,

**[01:04:30.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001567)  
yung mga PWDs,

**[01:04:32.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001568)  
paano po yun?

**[01:04:34.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001569)  
Yun ho sana.

**[01:04:35.580] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001570)  
Do we have plans, Secretary, in the future siguro?

**[01:04:38.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001571)  
Very near future?

**[01:04:40.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001572)  
Baguhin natin yung structure na yan?

**[01:04:42.400] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001573)  
Magsayang na naman tayo ng pera?

**[01:04:44.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001574)  
Yes, Mr. Chair.

**[01:04:46.440] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001575)  
Insofar as the Puerto Princesa airport is concerned po,

**[01:04:49.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001576)  
ano naman ito for PPP natin ito by next year?

**[01:04:52.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001577)  
At ito po ay isa sa mga magiging requisitos po natin

**[01:04:55.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001578)  
at conditions.

**[01:04:56.360] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001579)  
At ito po ay isa sa mga requisitos po natin

**[01:04:57.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001580)  
for PPP na gawa ng paraan

**[01:05:00.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001581)  
para at least maaralin nila mabuti po

**[01:05:03.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001582)  
kung paano natin malagyan ito ng ating PPP,

**[01:05:06.840] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001583)  
yung passenger boarding bridge.

**[01:05:09.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001584)  
Kung paano gawin.

**[01:05:10.580] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001585)  
So yan naman po, moving forward nga po sinasabi natin ngayon

**[01:05:13.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001586)  
na lahat na po ng ating magiging disenyo,

**[01:05:17.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001587)  
pag hindi lang po pagdating sa terminal po,

**[01:05:19.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001588)  
dapat nakahanda na, kung bakit nakaabang na po

**[01:05:22.460] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001589)  
na dapat may passenger boarding bridge.

**[01:05:25.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001590)  
And hindi lang po yan, if I may add po,

**[01:05:27.420] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001591)  
ganun na rin po pagdating sa ating runway.

**[01:05:30.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001592)  
Kahit maliit na airport niyan,

**[01:05:32.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001593)  
o konti lang yung pasahero,

**[01:05:33.320] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001594)  
dapat ang runway natin minimum 2,100 na.

**[01:05:37.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001595)  
Bilhin na natin ngayon yung lupa,

**[01:05:39.380] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001596)  
kasi kapag yan ginawa natin 1-4, 1-7,

**[01:05:42.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001597)  
eh ganun din naman po pagdating ng araw,

**[01:05:45.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001598)  
baka kailangan natin na mas mahabang runway.

**[01:05:47.660] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001599)  
So that would be the policy from now on, Mr. Chair.

**[01:05:49.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001600)  
So just to add, probably we have to make it a policy lahat,

**[01:05:53.600] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001601)  
especially all those international airports,

**[01:05:55.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001602)  
have to be, kailangan may tube yun.

**[01:05:58.620] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001603)  
Yung Puerto Princesa kasi, nakakapanghinayang,

**[01:06:01.160] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001604)  
Central Tulpo, bagong gawa, international airport,

**[01:06:04.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001605)  
eh dapat ginawa na natin ng tama.

**[01:06:07.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001606)  
So I think from now on, we need to,

**[01:06:10.500] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001607)  
all international airports have to be,

**[01:06:13.340] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001608)  
have to have provisions for tubes.

**[01:06:17.840] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001609)  
We agree, Mr. Chair.

**[01:06:19.040] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001610)  
Pati yung Lawag International Airport,

**[01:06:20.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001611)  
just in case we will improve on it later on,

**[01:06:23.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001612)  
kasi international airport din yun eh.

**[01:06:25.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001613)  
Wala rin, parang Puerto Princesa rin yun na walang tubes.

**[01:06:29.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001614)  
So thank you.

**[01:06:32.900] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001615)  
Kailangan nila aralin ngayon.

**[01:06:34.600] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001616)  
Maybe they're thinking about the design,

**[01:06:37.980] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001617)  
the facade, and more than the functionality po.

**[01:06:43.160] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001618)  
Pero putting a passenger boarding bridge,

**[01:06:46.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001619)  
it's not that costly po eh.

**[01:06:48.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001620)  
Ang computation namin dyan is just I think 50M.

**[01:06:51.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001621)  
I think they just have to study it po.

**[01:06:53.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001622)  
I don't know the structural design,

**[01:06:55.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001623)  
if it can carry that load itself.

**[01:06:58.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001624)  
Pero yan po ay magiging nga po tulad ng sinabi namin,

**[01:07:01.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001625)  
magiging pong parte ng mga condition at requisitos

**[01:07:05.060] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001626)  
para po pag sa PPP natin for next year

**[01:07:07.580] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001627)  
ng Puerto Princesa ko.

**[01:07:09.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001628)  
So Chair, I just have about three more questions.

**[01:07:12.660] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001629)  
Yung po sa Puerto Princesa,

**[01:07:14.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001630)  
kailan ho kaya natin mawapondohan po nito

**[01:07:16.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001631)  
kung babaguhin po natin yung airport?

**[01:07:20.640] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001632)  
Yung ating General Rosario.

**[01:07:23.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001633)  
The funding po, the 50M,

**[01:07:25.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001634)  
pag ilan yung boarding bridge na kakayanin po,

**[01:07:27.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001635)  
I think CAAP,

**[01:07:28.460] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001636)  
Corporate Operating Budget,

**[01:07:29.780] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001637)  
kakayanin ng CAAP.

**[01:07:31.320] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001638)  
Kailan lang nila aralin po yung kung saan ilalagay.

**[01:07:35.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001639)  
Baka mas malaki po sa 50M per boarding bridge

**[01:07:39.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001640)  
and we can program it in 2027

**[01:07:42.440] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001641)  
sa corporate funds ng CAAP.

**[01:07:44.900] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001642)  
Okay. Maraming salamat po.

**[01:07:47.920] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001643)  
Dito naman po tayo doon na sa presentation po ninyo kanina,

**[01:07:51.860] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001644)  
sa Ketari, yung railway sa Mindanao.

**[01:07:55.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001645)  
Are we talking to foreign companies probably who can finance it?

**[01:08:01.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001646)  
Kasi may mga kausap po ako when I was the chairman of our foreign relations,

**[01:08:05.360] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001647)  
I was able to talk to the French ambassador.

**[01:08:08.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001648)  
I'm sure maraming po interested po na ikang pumundo dyan,

**[01:08:12.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001649)  
ikang yung foreign assisted.

**[01:08:17.640] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001650)  
Meron ho ba kayong tinitignan ngayon para matapos po natin?

**[01:08:20.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001651)  
Hindi lang yung pondo natin,

**[01:08:21.940] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001652)  
kung itulungan ho tayo,

**[01:08:23.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001653)  
nababayaran naman natin kasi,

**[01:08:25.520] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001654)  
kung magiging entire Mindanao,

**[01:08:28.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001655)  
lagyan po natin,

**[01:08:29.200] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001656)  
not only in Davao,

**[01:08:30.380] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001657)  
kundi iikot po yan,

**[01:08:32.060] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001658)  
Cotabato,

**[01:08:32.960] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001659)  
hanggang Sambuanga,

**[01:08:34.420] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001660)  
Cagayan de Oro.

**[01:08:37.700] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001661)  
Yes, Mr. Chairman.

**[01:08:39.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001662)  
Before I give it to Yusef TJ,

**[01:08:41.680] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001663)  
doon sa mga ibang mga pwedeng tumulong sa atin,

**[01:08:45.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001664)  
pagpupondo dito,

**[01:08:46.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001665)  
just last week po,

**[01:08:48.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001666)  
kasama natin ang mahal na Pangulo dito sa India,

**[01:08:51.040] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001667)  
isa rin po yan,

**[01:08:52.060] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001668)  
yung Mindanao Rail project natin

**[01:08:54.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001669)  
that we pitched to,

**[01:08:55.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001670)  
to the government of India,

**[01:08:57.340] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001671)  
at they're interested naman po.

**[01:08:59.340] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001672)  
So, binibigyan natin talaga ng importansya

**[01:09:02.140] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001673)  
itong Mindanao Rail natin.

**[01:09:04.100] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001674)  
Ang sabi nga po ng Pangulo,

**[01:09:05.520] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001675)  
na talagang dapat yung mga infrastructure din natin,

**[01:09:08.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001676)  
tulad ng mga railways,

**[01:09:10.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001677)  
ay kailangan din po lumabas sa Metro Manila

**[01:09:12.560] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001678)  
at papunta na rin po sa Visayas at sa Mindanao.

**[01:09:15.900] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001679)  
So, I think Yusef TJ can also discuss the other,

**[01:09:19.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001680)  
kumbaga probable funders for our Mindanao Rail.

**[01:09:23.620] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001681)

**[01:09:39.820] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001690)

**[01:09:56.780] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-001697)

**[01:14:03.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001816)

**[01:14:08.240] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001818)

**[01:14:09.820] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001819)

**[01:14:13.800] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001821)

**[01:14:16.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001822)

**[01:14:18.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001823)

**[01:14:20.700] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001824)

**[01:14:25.180] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001826)

**[01:14:28.740] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001828)

**[01:14:30.560] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001829)

**[01:14:34.540] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001831)

**[01:14:36.080] Senator Erwin Tulfo** (asr-001832)

**[01:14:46.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001837)  
Nagaganda pa rin ang ating transportation

**[01:14:52.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001840)

**[01:14:54.320] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001841)

**[01:14:58.240] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001843)

**[01:15:00.000] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001844)

**[01:15:04.020] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001846)

**[01:15:07.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001848)

**[01:15:17.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001853)

**[01:15:25.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001857)

**[01:15:29.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001859)

**[01:15:31.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001860)

**[01:15:33.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001861)

**[01:15:35.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001862)

**[01:15:38.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001863)

**[01:15:39.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001864)

**[01:15:41.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001865)

**[01:15:44.240] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001866)

**[01:15:45.300] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001867)

**[01:15:47.840] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001868)

**[01:15:59.920] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001874)

**[01:16:05.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-001877)  
Kaya nga po kami po ay talagang we're quite

**[02:47:37.980] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004610)

**[02:47:58.340] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004620)

**[02:48:00.940] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004621)

**[02:48:04.820] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004623)

**[02:48:06.260] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004624)

**[02:48:08.540] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004625)

**[02:48:16.280] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004629)

**[02:48:18.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004630)

**[02:48:20.400] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004631)

**[02:48:21.860] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004632)

**[02:48:23.800] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004633)

**[02:48:30.440] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-004636)

**[03:03:51.500] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005012)  
transport drivers, transport operators, itong fuel subsidy.

**[03:03:56.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005013)  
At that time, ang ginagamit po namin yung 2025 budget na 2.5 billion.

**[03:04:03.720] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005014)  
At nung yan po ay naibigay na sa amin ng DBM, I think in the span of 3 months at the most, or 2 months po,

**[03:04:12.120] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005015)  
na-disperse po natin yung buong 2.5 billion.

**[03:04:15.760] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005016)  
At napakalaking tulong po yan sa ating mga transport group.

**[03:04:19.620] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005017)  
Ito po mula sa mga sinasabi nating regular jeepney, all the way po sa mga iba't-ibang,

**[03:04:26.740] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005018)  
modes ng transportation, maliban lang po sa tricycle, kasi hindi napapaloob sa LTFRB po yan.

**[03:04:33.860] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005019)  
So, makikiusap ulit po kami sa inyo, sa Committee on Finance, sa Senado at Kongreso,

**[03:04:40.300] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-005020)  
na sana mabigyan po kami ng budget para sa fuel subsidy para sa taong 2027, Mr. Chairman.

**[03:04:49.560] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005021)  
Salamat po, Secretary Banoy. Napakahalaga po ito sa ating mga super, lalong-lalo na po.

**[03:04:56.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005022)  
Ngayon, no, na biglang tumataas yung presyo ng krudo at ng gasolina, nabibigla sila.

**[03:05:04.160] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005023)  
Sana po handa ang ating gobyerno at patuloy po tayo makikiusap.

**[03:05:08.440] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005024)  
Taman-tama po yung mga kaharap nyo dyan, ating chairman ng Finance Committee, Senator JV,

**[03:05:16.480] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005025)  
and of course, Senate President Wynne Gatchalian, na dati rin po, last year siya yung chairman ng Committee on Finance.

**[03:05:23.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005026)  
So, sana po.

**[03:05:26.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005027)  
Sa mga kasama natin sa Senado, matulungan po kagadito na mabigyan rin po ng pondo para mas handa po ang ating DOTR.

**[03:05:36.680] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005028)  
Yun lang po, mga kasamahan ko. Salamat po sa inyo lahat, sa lahat po ng Secretary Banoy Lopez

**[03:05:43.620] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005029)  
at sa lahat po ng attached agencies, sa lahat po ng mga officials ng DOTR.

**[03:05:49.620] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005030)  
Salamat po sa inyong servisyo sa ating mga kababayan, lalong-lalo na po sa ngayon, no,

**[03:05:56.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005031)  
sa may krisis tayo sa pagtaas ng krudo, sana po'y maging bumalik sa dating normal na sitwasyon,

**[03:06:10.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005032)  
ating sitwasyon ng transport sector.

**[03:06:18.260] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005033)  
Magtulungan lang po tayo at unahin nyo po yung pagsiservisyo sa ating mga kababayan,

**[03:06:24.920] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005034)  
lalong-lalo na po yung mga mahihirap

**[03:06:26.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005035)  
nating kababayan.

**[03:06:27.900] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005036)  
Salamat po. Yun lang po sa Mr. Chair, yun lang po ang pakiusap ko sa inyo.

**[03:06:34.740] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005037)  
Ako naman ay full support sa ating, sa budget always ng DOTR ever since, ever since sinusuportahan po natin ito.

**[03:06:44.560] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005038)  
Sana po'y may isubmit lang po nila yung kanilang mga updates ng kanilang mga proyekto

**[03:06:54.320] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005039)  
to submit a definite and credible...

**[03:06:56.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005040)  
to submit a credible timetable for the implementation po ng mga proyekto na nakalaan for 2026

**[03:07:03.640] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005041)  
bago tayo mag-approve ng 2027 na budget.

**[03:07:11.500] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005042)  
Salamat po Mr. Chair. Salamat sa mga empleyado ng DOTR headed by Secretary Banoy Lopez.

**[03:07:18.720] Senator Christopher Lawrence T. Go** (asr-005043)  
Magandang hapon po.

**[03:07:20.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005044)  
Salamat eh, Senator Bogo.

**[03:07:21.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005045)  
So, the DOTR family, the agencies, baka ano lang, working lunch tayo.

**[03:07:26.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005046)  
So, para matapos tayo, we plan to finish hopefully by 2 p.m.

**[03:07:33.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005047)  
So, okay lang mag-working lunch, you can eat while we are continuing this hearing.

**[03:07:41.700] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005048)  
Okay, huwag lang nga ba nagsasalita.

**[03:07:44.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005049)  
Anyway, I'd like to take advantage kanina na nag-ring up ng Senate President natin tungkol sa

**[03:07:51.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005050)  
tungkol sa Stratcom LTMS.

**[03:07:54.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005051)  
Nandito na rin lang si...

**[03:07:56.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005052)  
Asek Marcos, na kanilaw, it was...

**[03:08:01.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005053)  
Kanina partially binanggit, ano na rin, it was discussed by your Executive Director.

**[03:08:05.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005054)  
Pero siguro in a nutshell, yung Stratcom, ano na, issued a memorandum already.

**[03:08:14.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005055)  
Recently, cancelling and LTMS na tayo.

**[03:08:17.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005056)  
Tsaka ano yung needs siya po mamaya?

**[03:08:20.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005057)  
Please, sige, Asek, na kanilaw, habang nandito yung Senate President.

**[03:08:24.340] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005058)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair. Good afternoon po.

**[03:08:26.500] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005059)  
Your Honors, unang-unang po, yes, sir.

**[03:08:31.240] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005060)  
Yun pong Stratcom, ay naglabas po kami last week ng kautosan doon na totally sinarado na po namin yan.

**[03:08:40.480] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005061)  
So, hindi na po natin ginagamit ang Stratcom kasi isa rin po yan na nagpahirap sa atin sa mahabang panahon,

**[03:08:47.100] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005062)  
for 23 years, yung 169 pesos na sinacharge sa atin.

**[03:08:51.900] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005063)  
So, ngayon po, fully utilized na po natin ang LTMS.

**[03:08:56.500] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005064)  
Kaya yun po yung aming hinihiling po sana na mas masuportahan po natin yung,

**[03:09:02.160] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005065)  
especially po yung mga kailangan naming budget for LTMS.

**[03:09:07.420] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005066)  
Kasi mahabang panahon, sir, hindi po siya nabigyan po ng budget.

**[03:09:12.360] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005067)  
Especially po itong establishment of disaster recovery center,

**[03:09:17.060] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005068)  
kung ating mga data, and also the maintenance and support services for the existing LTMS.

**[03:09:24.160] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005069)  
Hindi po siya nabigyan ng maintenance before.

**[03:09:26.500] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005070)  
So, napaka-importante po na mas ma-maintain po natin siya.

**[03:09:30.960] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005071)  
At saka po yung mga hardware refresh natin sa LTMS.

**[03:09:38.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005072)  
Pagkano yung nire-request nyo, pahabol to, wala ito sa NEP.

**[03:09:44.280] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005073)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:09:45.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005074)  
This is for the, just for the record, is for the maintenance or yung pong aside from the establishment, yung recovery?

**[03:09:54.400] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005075)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:09:55.260] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005076)  
First po.

**[03:09:56.500] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005077)  
Yung establishment ng disaster recovery center for existing LTMS.

**[03:10:01.180] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005078)  
Kasi yung data po natin doon para may backup po tayo.

**[03:10:04.780] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005079)  
Maintenance and support service for the existing LTMS.

**[03:10:07.620] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005080)  
Kasi wala pong naging maintenance na budget sa mahabang panahon.

**[03:10:13.140] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005081)  
Yung refresh po ng LTMS, ganun din po.

**[03:10:16.660] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005082)  
Wala pong nabigyan ng refresh.

**[03:10:19.180] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005083)  
E samantalang ginagamit po natin siya ang LTMS.

**[03:10:21.980] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005084)  
And fully utilized po natin.

**[03:10:24.380] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005085)  
And we also requested,

**[03:10:26.500] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005086)  
we requested for confidential funds for our operation against,

**[03:10:32.820] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005087)  
alam naman po natin, ang LTO, kami yung humaharap sa mga kolorong,

**[03:10:38.020] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005088)  
may mga organized crime po yan eh.

**[03:10:40.040] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005089)  
So talagang mas tinututukan po namin.

**[03:10:44.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005090)  
So for the record, yung LTMS is now being run by our LTO personnel?

**[03:10:50.380] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005091)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:10:51.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005092)  
Yung Dermalog, are they still on board?

**[03:10:54.620] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005093)  
Ngayon po, sir.

**[03:10:56.500] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005094)  
Inaayos po namin, kasi yung maintenance, sir, importante po na, kasi sila po yung nagprograma niyan.

**[03:11:03.320] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005095)  
Importante na magtuloy-tuloy, kasi mas maaalalayan po nila ang LTO.

**[03:11:09.740] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005096)  
Kasi sa kanila po, sila po nag-formulate po niyan eh.

**[03:11:12.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005097)  
So parang support services?

**[03:11:15.280] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005098)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:11:16.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005099)  
Seth, our sent president, baka you might want to ask.

**[03:11:21.920] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005100)  
Ah, Sek, nakita ko dito, no, yung value,

**[03:11:26.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005101)  
presyo ng LTMS is about 3.1 billion.

**[03:11:30.520] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005102)  
And I agree with you na doesn't make sense na binayaran natin isang taon na

**[03:11:36.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005103)  
ang Stratcom ng 3 billion.

**[03:11:40.260] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005104)  
There was one time, kanina pinakita ko na I think in 2019,

**[03:11:44.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005105)  
one time binayaran natin 3 billion.

**[03:11:46.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005106)  
2020, 3 billion ulit.

**[03:11:48.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005107)  
2021, 3 billion ulit.

**[03:11:50.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005108)  
Imagine mo, yung cost ng isang software, 3.1, pero binayad natin sa kanya,

**[03:11:56.240] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005109)  
one year lang, is 3 billion.

**[03:12:00.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005110)  
Ngayon ang tanong ko, iba kasi yung model ni Stratcom eh.

**[03:12:04.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005111)  
Yung model nito ngayon, kay Dermalog.

**[03:12:07.900] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005112)  
Pero in this case, pag tinignan nyo yung feasibility nitong model na ginawa natin,

**[03:12:16.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005113)  
is it much economical compared to Stratcom?

**[03:12:20.240] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005114)  
Yes, sir. Mas economical po siya, SP.

**[03:12:24.680] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005115)  
Okay.

**[03:12:26.240] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005116)  
And Mr. Chair, mas advanced pa po.

**[03:12:29.740] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005117)  
In fact, sir, kung matandaan po ninyo, nung nagkaroon ako ng crackdown sa mga smuggled na sports car,

**[03:12:40.040] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005118)  
madali namin nalolocate, sir, sa LTMS at hindi kasi nakakapasok, sir, sa LTMS.

**[03:12:44.740] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005119)  
So isang dahilan yun, sir, na and also sa mga OFW po natin,

**[03:12:49.260] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005120)  
nari-renew ko ho yung mga driver's license nila abroad.

**[03:12:52.940] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005121)  
Hindi kagaya ho ng Stratcom, sir.

**[03:12:55.580] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005122)  
Na wala silang mga ganong facility or ganong sistema para mas mapadali po ang pagsiservisyo po natin sa mga tao.

**[03:13:06.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005123)  
Itong Dermalog is a German company?

**[03:13:08.880] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005124)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:13:09.500] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005125)  
Na ang kanyang specialization is biometrics?

**[03:13:15.100] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005126)  
Yes, sir. Marami ho silang mga clients abroad, especially in Malaysia.

**[03:13:19.320] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005127)  
Naging maayos naman po ang kanilang mga…

**[03:13:22.340] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005128)  
So merong proof of concept, in other words.

**[03:13:24.200] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005129)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:13:24.780] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005130)  
Oo nga.

**[03:13:25.580] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005131)  
Yung LTMS, sir, so far, kaya very vocal po ako dyan.

**[03:13:28.780] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005132)  
Wala tayong naging problema.

**[03:13:31.220] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005133)  
In fact, mas naging advanced tayo, lalo na sa ating mga driver's license at yung mga registration ng sasakyan.

**[03:13:38.840] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005134)  
Nawawala po yung mga… wala sa ayos.

**[03:13:43.300] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005135)  
Especially po yung mga Chinese driver's license na locate po namin through LTMS, biometrics.

**[03:13:49.420] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005136)  
So ganon siya kahalaga, sir.

**[03:13:51.460] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005137)  
Chinese driver's license, what is that?

**[03:13:55.580] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005138)  
Before kasi, sir, alam naman po…

**[03:13:57.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005139)  
Sa Pogo?

**[03:13:57.460] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005140)  
Yes, sir. Before, nakakita naman natin nung very active po si, sir, nung time ng Pogo, si General Cruz.

**[03:14:09.100] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005141)  
Si Gilbert nag-crackdown ng Pogo, eh.

**[03:14:11.780] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005142)  
Yes, sir. Marami ho kaming nakita na through LTMS kasi, sir, yung biometrics, yung last operation po namin, na-determine namin na yung Filipino name, e meron Chinese pala siya.

**[03:14:23.700] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005143)  
So kasi ang ginawa nila dati, sir.

**[03:14:25.580] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005144)  
Chinese talaga yung kumukuha. Ngayon nung ito na, ang ginawa nila, nagpalit sila ng Filipino name. Pero doon sa biometrics, nakita po namin through LTMS.

**[03:14:36.380] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005145)  
Okay. Okay. Paano itong… Last question, Mr. Chairman. Paano itong… Kasi tinitignan ko sa news. Paano itong mga… There's an effort to refund the 13 billion?

**[03:14:50.140] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005146)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:14:50.660] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005147)  
I don't know if kasama kayo, but I read in the news na may mga groups na nagpa-file ng cases to get the refund

**[03:15:00.000] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005148)  
Yes, sir. Kasi nga, yun po yung mahabang panahon na it was charged for the P169, sir. So ngayon, sir, nasa ano naman po natin, legality, atin sa humingi po kami ng tulong sa DOTR para po mas maayayos po natin yung tungkol po dyan.

**[03:15:16.620] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005149)  
Ang tawag nila, Coalition 169.

**[03:15:18.680] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005150)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:15:19.480] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005151)  
And they're trying to refund P28 billion.

**[03:15:23.100] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005152)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:15:23.640] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005153)  
Kaya ba ma-refund yun?

**[03:15:24.880] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005154)  
Hindi ko po masagot ngayon yan, sir. Pero pinatitingnan po namin yung legality at saka kung paano po.

**[03:15:31.080] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005155)  
Hindi ko rin alam bakit po sa mahabang panahon kasi naganyan yung mga kababayan natin.

**[03:15:36.180] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005156)  
So kawawa po. Sa totoo lang, mabigat po talaga yung P169. Nakala kasi sa iba magaan pero sa masa, mabigat po yun.

**[03:15:46.940] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005157)  
But pag sinuma mo yan, biro mo P3 billion a year.

**[03:15:50.360] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005158)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:15:50.760] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005159)  
That's the exact cost of the system na binili nyo.

**[03:15:53.720] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005160)  
Yes, sir.

**[03:15:54.100] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005161)  
Magagamit natin.

**[03:15:54.880] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005162)  
Magagamit natin sa mahabang panahon.

**[03:15:56.120] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005163)  
And mas maayos pa po yung sistema, sir.

**[03:15:58.140] Senator Sherwin "Win" T. Gatchalian** (asr-005164)  
Thank you. Thank you, Asec.

**[03:16:00.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005165)  
Thank you, our Senate President.

**[03:16:04.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005166)  
So yung additional request, Asec, for the maintenance and yung pag-setup ng inyong recovery disaster, how much is that?

**[03:16:16.160] LTO Assistant Secretary Markus V. Lacanilao** (asr-005167)

**[03:31:19.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005639)

**[03:31:21.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005640)

**[03:32:07.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005650)

**[03:32:25.360] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005654)

**[03:32:26.520] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005655)

**[03:32:30.340] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005657)  
Para po doon sa 28 kilometers, 7 stations po na Valenzuela to Malolos.

**[03:32:36.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005658)

**[03:32:38.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005659)

**[03:32:43.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-005660)

**[03:32:47.680] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005661)  
Mr. Chair, overall po yung buong North-South Commuter Rail ay nasa 78% po na right-of-way.

**[03:32:53.300] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005662)  
Yun pong N1 segment natin, yung sa gitna po, ay nasa 99% na po tayo.

**[03:33:00.080] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005663)  
So, ito po yung kasama po yung magbubukas po next year.

**[03:33:04.180] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005664)  
Yun pong N2 segment, yung North-South segment po natin papuntang Clark, ay nasa 86% po tayo.

**[03:33:10.620] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005665)  
Kailangan po po natin ng mga 2 billion para po doon sa papuntang Clark.

**[03:33:15.020] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005666)  
At doon po sa South natin, yung mula po Maynila papuntang Calamba, ay nasa 64% pa lang po tayo.

**[03:33:22.680] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005667)  
Actually,

**[03:33:23.300] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005668)

**[03:33:32.780] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005669)

**[03:33:40.100] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005670)

**[03:33:43.160] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005671)

**[03:33:53.300] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005672)

**[03:33:59.220] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005673)

**[03:34:07.320] DOTr Undersecretary Timothy John Batan** (asr-005674)
