**[04:44:37.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007170)  
I think

**[04:44:38.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007171)  
si Senator Tulfo

**[04:44:39.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007172)  
na mayroong mga substation

**[04:44:40.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007173)  
na hindi nabigyan

**[04:44:41.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007174)  
ng budget allocation

**[04:44:42.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007175)  
for next year.

**[04:44:44.480] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007176)  
Ilan ba ito?

**[04:44:45.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007177)  
Dalawa o tatlo?

**[04:44:46.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007178)  
May epekto ba ito

**[04:44:46.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007179)  
sa posture natin

**[04:44:47.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007180)  
sa West Philippine Sea?

**[04:44:48.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007181)  
Magkano itong

**[04:44:49.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007182)  
substations

**[04:44:50.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007183)  
na walang budget allocation?

**[04:44:54.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007184)  
Lalo yung sa West Philippine Sea?

**[04:44:56.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007185)  
Meron?

**[04:44:56.700] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007186)  
Good afternoon.

**[04:44:59.280] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007187)  
Ah,

**[04:45:00.000] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007210)  
Sir Chairman, Sir, there are still eight stations in need of funding.

**[04:45:06.840] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007211)  
So, 25 million each po yan.

**[04:45:10.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007212)  
So, West Philippine Sea, di ba?

**[04:45:12.200] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007213)  
It will, it will, yes, His Excellency, Palawan area and KIG.

**[04:45:18.760] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007214)  
And obviously, it will have a very significant impact to our activities in the West Philippine Sea.

**[04:45:27.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007215)  
Does any update sa media? May patanong, may update ba?

**[04:45:33.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007216)  
Anong update sa pagbangga ng Chinese vessel sa Bifar vessel? Kanina umaga ba ito?

**[04:45:39.020] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007217)  
Yes, at 11.15 today, one Chinese Coast Guard vessel rammed a Bifar vessel.

**[04:45:47.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007218)  
Anong circumstances? May nasaktan ba sa atin?

**[04:45:50.000] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007219)  
Well, there are no personnel injuries.

**[04:45:52.980] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007220)  
They're very, ano ito, aggressive na.

**[04:45:55.840] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007221)  
May damage yung radio.

**[04:45:57.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007222)  
May killings ng barko.

**[04:45:59.380] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007223)  
A Philippine Coast Guard vessel is now proceeding to the area to assist.

**[04:46:04.040] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007224)  
And they also dispatched an aircraft to the area.

**[04:46:07.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007225)  
Hindi naman, ano, na-disabled ba yung, ano, yung Bifar vessel?

**[04:46:10.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007226)  
Hindi naman.

**[04:46:13.320] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007227)  
Actually, Your Honor, yesterday, we also assisted the same vessel, the same Bifar vessel.

**[04:46:21.660] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007228)  
Na-aground siya yesterday.

**[04:46:24.400] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007229)  
Nabunot natin.

**[04:46:25.640] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007230)  
Then, the captain...

**[04:46:27.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007231)  
He decided to proceed with the mission of giving fuel subsidies to the fishermen.

**[04:46:33.300] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007232)  
Meron po tayong tinatawag natin kadiwa para sa mga bagong bayaning manging isda or KBBM,

**[04:46:42.880] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007233)  
where the Coast Guard and Bifar gives fuel subsidies, ice, water, and goodies to fishermen to encourage them to fish offshore.

**[04:46:55.640] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007234)  
So, ginagawa natin...

**[04:46:57.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007235)  
May action ba tayo sa ganitong mga incidents sa West Philippine Sea?

**[04:47:01.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007236)  
Pag merong ganitong intimidation sa Bifar, sa mga fishermen, what does the Coast Guard take action?

**[04:47:11.040] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007237)  
For one, we already issued the press release.

**[04:47:15.900] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007238)  
We have been banking on information as our strongest, the most potent weapon in this kind of game we're in.

**[04:47:27.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007239)  
So, we have released the PR already.

**[04:47:29.200] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007240)  
We are informing the world that China has behaved this way again.

**[04:47:35.840] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007241)  
Saka ano na to, talagang they're still becoming very, very hostile and aggressive na itong they rammed our Bifar vessel.

**[04:47:47.320] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007242)  
Yes, Mr. Chairman.

**[04:47:50.460] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007243)  
We have to remember that Bifar vessel is manned by civilian sailors, actually.

**[04:47:57.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007244)  
Hindi po yan armed.

**[04:47:59.860] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007245)  
It's just there to distribute support to our fishermen.

**[04:48:05.280] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007246)  
So, it is very unfortunate to see a public vessel treated like that by China Coast Guard.

**[04:48:14.100] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007247)  
Ano na ito, no?

**[04:48:14.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007248)  
Talagang this is really a disrespect to our sovereignty.

**[04:48:20.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007249)  
And wala nang respeto talaga, eh, no?

**[04:48:23.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007250)  
So, did you...

**[04:48:24.960] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007251)  
Also, every time...

**[04:48:27.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007252)  
There's an incident.

**[04:48:29.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007253)  
Do you report this to the DFA para magsulat ng diplomatic protest?

**[04:48:34.900] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007254)  
We are part of the National Task Force on the West Philippine Sea and DFA is part of it.

**[04:48:41.180] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007255)  
Your Honor.

**[04:48:44.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007256)  
Okay.

**[04:48:45.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007257)  
Considering the 57.5% cut to our capital outlays and the 3.3 billion reduction in air,

**[04:48:57.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007258)  
and floating asset acquisition and maintenance,

**[04:49:00.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007259)  
medyo malaki pala, ano, no?

**[04:49:02.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007260)  
How will the PCG maintain adequate sea-based patrols in the critical areas like West Philippine Sea?

**[04:49:09.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007261)  
So, 3.3 billion pa na ang reduction natin from the NEP for the acquisition of air and floating assets.

**[04:49:18.980] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007262)  
It will significantly compromise our operational ability, Your Honor.

**[04:49:25.480] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007263)  
We are already operating...

**[04:49:27.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007264)  
at the maximum.

**[04:49:30.420] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007265)  
The repair and maintenance requirement of our ships is already at the, you know,

**[04:49:37.400] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007266)  
delikado na po.

**[04:49:39.660] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007267)  
Baka din na namin masustain.

**[04:49:41.200] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007268)  
So, we are really appealing to the Senate to consider our budgetary request.

**[04:49:49.980] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007269)  
We have to remember, Your Honor, that we only have so much ships.

**[04:49:56.400] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007270)  
And operating...

**[04:49:57.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007271)  
at almost 80-90% operational tempo.

**[04:50:01.540] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007272)  
So, the time between overhaul is very, very short.

**[04:50:06.880] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007273)  
It's just a matter of time that we will run out of ships.

**[04:50:11.020] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007274)  
We are trying to prevent that.

**[04:50:12.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007275)  
Do we have any vessel expected to be coming?

**[04:50:16.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007276)  
I think yung mga binring up ni Senator Zubiri,

**[04:50:21.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007277)  
President Zubiri before, yung naglagay tayo ng 2.5 billion na hindi na tuloy.

**[04:50:26.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007278)  
So, what are the updates?

**[04:50:27.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007279)  
What's the status of these vessels?

**[04:50:30.500] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007280)  
Yun pong 2.5 billion na hindi po natuloy yung nung 2024-25.

**[04:50:39.180] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007281)  
Nagpapasalamat po kami.

**[04:50:40.280] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007282)  
Pinunduhan niyo po kami uli.

**[04:50:42.300] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007283)  
So, we are now pursuing the procurement process for the local construction of 160-meter vessel.

**[04:50:51.500] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007284)  
But on top of that, we are...

**[04:50:53.900] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007285)  
We are to receive 5 more...

**[04:50:56.960] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007286)  
97...

**[04:50:57.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007287)  
7 meters from Japan.

**[04:51:00.420] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007288)  
And 40-35 meter fast patrol crafts from France.

**[04:51:06.900] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007289)  
But this will happen at the soonest next year, yung maliliit.

**[04:51:13.300] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007290)  
And yung malalaki will be 2029.

**[04:51:17.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007291)  
So, sa ngayon po, Your Honor, we have to make good with what we have.

**[04:51:22.100] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007292)  
That's why we really need very, very badly

**[04:51:25.140] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007293)  
the repair and maintenance.

**[04:51:27.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007294)  
Funding.

**[04:51:30.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007295)  
Kaya nga.

**[04:51:30.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007296)  
So, itong drop in sea-based patrol funding under the Maritime Security Law Enforcement Program,

**[04:51:35.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007297)  
it will really impact yung PCG's daily operational response, no?

**[04:51:40.100] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007298)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:51:41.400] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007299)  
Assaults.

**[04:51:42.600] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007300)  
It's the minimum that we need.

**[04:51:44.540] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007301)  
So, kung babawasan pa po, we will be constrained.

**[04:51:48.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007302)  
It was cut down by 812 million.

**[04:51:52.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007303)  
Ano po yung bare minimum para naman at least yung presence sa atin ay nandun,

**[04:51:56.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007304)  
hindi ma-hamper yung...

**[04:51:57.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007305)  
I can't give you the numbers now, Your Honor, but we can give it back to you.

**[04:52:00.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007306)  
Yes, please submit yung talagang ano.

**[04:52:02.620] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007307)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:52:02.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007308)  
Again, bare minimum.

**[04:52:04.160] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007309)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:52:04.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007310)  
Pasensya na, talagang masikip ang ating space ngayon,

**[04:52:07.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007311)  
kaya lahat ang hinihingi ko sa lahat ng agencies now is bare minimum.

**[04:52:11.220] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007312)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:52:11.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007313)  
Just to survive.

**[04:52:13.100] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007314)  
If I may, Your Honor, take advantage of this opportunity.

**[04:52:16.980] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007315)  
We also submitted a supplemental request.

**[04:52:21.020] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007316)  
And we listed there the key activities that we must undertake.

**[04:52:25.500] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007317)  
Okay.

**[04:52:26.280] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007318)  
Number one is the...

**[04:52:27.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007319)  
Continuation of our personal procurement program,

**[04:52:30.640] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007320)  
which is in our table of organization and equipage.

**[04:52:35.360] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007321)  
Because if we fail in undertaking that activity,

**[04:52:39.920] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007322)  
the pyramid structure of our organization will be compromised.

**[04:52:43.940] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007323)  
And it will result in incongruence in the promotion of our people,

**[04:52:50.420] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007324)  
and that can affect retention.

**[04:52:52.560] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007325)  
It can affect morale.

**[04:52:54.140] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007326)  
The last thing that we need if we are to keep the Coast Guard,

**[04:52:57.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007327)  
that disciplined and in high morale to secure the West Philippine Sea.

**[04:53:03.040] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007328)  
Second is we also forwarded to this body a request

**[04:53:08.880] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007329)  
to undertake at least our own approach in the acquisition of helicopters.

**[04:53:16.000] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007330)  
Your Honor, we only have one helicopter flying now,

**[04:53:18.600] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007331)  
the one that we are using in Coron.

**[04:53:20.880] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007332)  
The other one needs major repair.

**[04:53:25.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007333)  
And number three,

**[04:53:27.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007334)  
to leverage automated systems, unmanned systems,

**[04:53:34.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007335)  
artificial intelligence in the way we do things.

**[04:53:37.120] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007336)  
So we have also placed there unmanned tactical capabilities.

**[04:53:43.040] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007337)  
We'd like to have this innovation in the way we do things

**[04:53:48.760] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007338)  
because we wish to be somehow...

**[04:53:53.040] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007339)  
to remain within the curve,

**[04:53:56.040] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007340)  
to remain within the race.

**[04:53:57.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007341)  
With AI now being employed by our adversaries.

**[04:54:01.400] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007342)  
We just can't remain in the conventional way

**[04:54:04.160] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007343)  
if we are to keep this country safe.

**[04:54:09.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007344)  
So for the record,

**[04:54:10.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007345)  
the Coast Guard only has one operating air asset or chopper.

**[04:54:17.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007346)  
Chopper.

**[04:54:18.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007347)  
As you mentioned.

**[04:54:20.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007348)  
How many choppers do we have?

**[04:54:21.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007349)  
Just two?

**[04:54:22.120] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007350)  
Just two.

**[04:54:22.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007351)  
So we are 50%.

**[04:54:24.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007352)  
Where do you want to go?

**[04:54:27.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007353)  
Nasa Palawan area dito.

**[04:54:29.440] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007354)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:54:29.860] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007355)  
We dispatch it there when we have this recent incident.

**[04:54:36.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007356)  
So your proposal is to acquire additional air asset

**[04:54:41.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007357)  
or to repair the other one?

**[04:54:43.520] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007358)  
Yung repair po, may funding na po tayo

**[04:54:45.680] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007359)  
nasa procurement.

**[04:54:47.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007360)  
Air bus yan eh, no?

**[04:54:49.160] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007361)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:54:49.800] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007362)  
Pero yung...

**[04:54:50.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007363)  
Kamahal ng air bus kasi.

**[04:54:51.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007364)  
Opo.

**[04:54:51.860] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007365)  
But we are availing of this innovative way

**[04:54:56.680] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007366)  
of acquiring new assets.

**[04:54:59.460] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007367)  
It is a lease to own.

**[04:55:01.380] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007368)  
And with a performance-based logistics system attached to it,

**[04:55:06.720] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007369)  
we will be assured of around 80% availability of aircraft.

**[04:55:12.580] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007370)  
So it is a system now being pursued by other modern armed forces.

**[04:55:18.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007371)  
You are already pursuing this para madagdagan niya ating air asset?

**[04:55:22.480] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007372)  
Yes, Your Honor.

**[04:55:23.840] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007373)  
At saka mas mabilis po yung delivery, Your Honor.

**[04:55:26.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007374)  
Also, meron kayong cut.

**[04:55:31.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007375)  
There's a 600 million reduction in the unmanned tactical capability project?

**[04:55:37.040] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007376)  
Yes, it's the...

**[04:55:38.900] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007377)  
We requested for that.

**[04:55:40.120] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007378)  
Dito pong binanggit ko po.

**[04:55:41.960] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007379)  
Hindi po siya na-consider sa NEP namin.

**[04:55:44.300] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007380)  
So we'd like to appeal.

**[04:55:45.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007381)  
Ano tayo? Ito yung mga drones?

**[04:55:47.380] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007382)  
Yes, air, surface, and undersea.

**[04:55:50.400] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007383)  
Undersea.

**[04:55:50.900] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007384)  
Undersea.

**[04:55:53.660] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007385)  
With regards to intelligence fund,

**[04:55:55.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007386)  
PCG's intelligence fund,

**[04:55:56.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007387)  
was reduced by 150 million for the current year

**[04:56:00.500] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007388)  
with only 400 million for 2027.

**[04:56:05.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007389)  
Is this enough for PCG?

**[04:56:07.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007390)  
What are the plans to optimize the amount?

**[04:56:11.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007391)  
We can discuss it in February.

**[04:56:16.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007392)  
Then the establishment of the Philippine Coast Guard Headquarters

**[04:56:19.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007393)  
is allocated with 1.7 billion for 2020

**[04:56:24.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007394)  
out of 5 billion total cost.

**[04:56:26.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007395)  
Can you provide us with the timeline for completion?

**[04:56:30.440] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007396)  
It will be completed in three years, Your Honor.

**[04:56:35.860] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007397)  
Ongoing? It's ongoing?

**[04:56:37.280] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007398)  
We will be doing our groundbreaking.

**[04:56:38.920] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007399)  
Our groundbreaking pala?

**[04:56:39.940] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007400)  
Yes, this month.

**[04:56:41.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007401)  
Okay.

**[04:56:42.400] PCG Commandant Admiral Ronnie Gil L. Gavan** (asr-007402)  
Hopefully with the President, Your Honor.

**[04:56:45.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007403)  
Okay, next.

**[04:56:48.600] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007404)  
Doon po sa OTS,

**[04:56:50.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007405)  
nasa Yusef Gilbert Cruz.

**[04:56:54.720] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007406)  
Doon pa sa mga main...

**[04:56:56.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007407)  
Ano natin, gateways sa yung ating mga especially na IA.

**[04:57:03.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007408)  
Okay naman ang ating mga X-ray system

**[04:57:06.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007409)  
kasi we do not want a delay.

**[04:57:07.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007410)  
We want better.

**[04:57:10.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007411)  
Yan ang ating first and last impression, of course.

**[04:57:13.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007412)  
Sige yung sa mga X-rays nyo.

**[04:57:16.880] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007413)  
Thank you, Mr. Chair.

**[04:57:18.760] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007414)  
Sir, I think it is necessary to strengthen the security capability

**[04:57:23.120] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007415)  
of our country's airport, sir.

**[04:57:25.220] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007416)  
And that is to ensure

**[04:57:26.680] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007417)  
adequate preparedness.

**[04:57:30.800] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007418)  
Especially, sir, yung mga international events po natin.

**[04:57:33.520] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007419)  
Meron tayo, sir, international events ngayon

**[04:57:35.460] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007420)  
that started last January 1st.

**[04:57:38.080] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007421)  
And ang pool blast niyan, sir.

**[04:57:39.980] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007422)  
And yung summit will be on November this year, sir.

**[04:57:45.000] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007423)  
So what we are doing right now, sir,

**[04:57:46.940] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007424)  
is pinagpapasa-pasa lang namin

**[04:57:49.660] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007425)  
yung mga security equipments natin, sir.

**[04:57:52.340] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007426)  
Mga X-ray machines.

**[04:57:53.580] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007427)  
Doon sa mga venues.

**[04:57:54.700] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007428)  
And ang nakikita,

**[04:57:56.680] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007429)  
nakikita namin, sir,

**[04:57:57.520] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007430)  
if we can buy more equipment,

**[04:57:59.920] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007431)  
modern equipment,

**[04:58:00.960] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007432)  
screening equipment, sir,

**[04:58:02.240] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007433)  
I think hindi na po tayo kailangan mag...

**[04:58:05.520] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007434)  
Parang nag-che-che-che-s kami, sir.

**[04:58:06.880] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007435)  
Kung may kailangan dito,

**[04:58:08.420] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007436)  
maglalagay kami, sir, ng screen.

**[04:58:09.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007437)  
So nililipat-lipat nila minsan yung equipment?

**[04:58:12.280] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007438)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:58:12.660] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007439)  
Actually, itong Senate office po natin,

**[04:58:15.180] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007440)  
nabigyan po namin ng isang screen X-ray machine, sir,

**[04:58:18.740] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007441)  
nung nangangailangan po kayo

**[04:58:19.920] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007442)  
during nung nagkaroon po ng problema dito, sir.

**[04:58:23.460] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007443)  
But yung mga naiya,

**[04:58:24.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007444)  
I think may mga bago ng ano, no?

**[04:58:26.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007445)  
I can see that there are new X-ray machines.

**[04:58:30.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007446)  
So you have a modernization program, no?

**[04:58:33.320] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007447)  
Yes, sir.

**[04:58:34.140] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007448)  
Tama po, sir.

**[04:58:34.840] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007449)  
But we have 95 airports, sir.

**[04:58:37.260] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007450)  
And 41 of those 95, sir,

**[04:58:39.520] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007451)  
yun po yung working.

**[04:58:40.580] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007452)  
And we are catering 9 international airports, sir.

**[04:58:44.840] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007453)  
And nagpapalaki pa po tayo ng mga airports ngayon, sir.

**[04:58:48.000] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007454)  
So I think we need additional security equipment for that.

**[04:58:52.980] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007455)  
And not only that, sir,

**[04:58:55.560] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007456)  
yung mga personnel,

**[04:58:56.680] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007457)  
like for example, sir,

**[04:59:00.060] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007458)  
meron po tayong...

**[04:59:01.780] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007459)  
We have a total of 2,247 screeners, sir.

**[04:59:06.980] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007460)  
Ito, sir, yung nakikita nyo ng naka-gray

**[04:59:09.000] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007461)  
na nag-aasikaso po dun sa mga screening aparatos natin.

**[04:59:15.280] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007462)  
And 875 of those,

**[04:59:19.200] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007463)  
SG-4 lang, sir.

**[04:59:21.580] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007464)  
Pag sinabi nating SG-4, sir,

**[04:59:23.960] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007465)  
ang sahod po nito ay 17,500.

**[04:59:26.680] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007466)  
to 18,000. So, napakaliit po, sir.

**[04:59:29.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007467)

**[04:59:31.220] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007468)

**[04:59:33.500] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007469)  
Yung majority po dyan, sir,

**[04:59:35.500] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007470)  
SG4 and SG8 lang,

**[04:59:37.360] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007471)  
sir. Yung 1,004

**[04:59:39.080] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007472)  
SG8 lang po yun, sir.

**[04:59:41.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007473)  
Ano lang yung sweldo po noon,

**[04:59:43.440] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007474)  
sir? Nasa 18,000. SG4?

**[04:59:45.380] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007475)  
Ang yung mga screeners? SG4 and 8S

**[04:59:47.320] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007476)  
hanggang SG8, sir.

**[04:59:49.560] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007477)  
Parang mataas pa yung

**[04:59:50.740] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007478)  
utility namin dito.

**[04:59:53.820] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007479)  
SG4, did you request

**[04:59:55.060] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007480)  
DBM for an

**[04:59:56.180] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007481)  
to upgrade

**[04:59:59.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007482)  
or to improve?

**[05:00:00.000] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007483)  
itong salaries

**[05:00:02.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007484)  
itong mga screeners?

**[05:00:03.780] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007485)  
Meron po kaming letters, sir, na pinadala.

**[05:00:06.340] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007486)  
And with this, sir...

**[05:00:07.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007487)  
How many are we talking about?

**[05:00:08.900] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007488)  
Ilan itong mga screeners

**[05:00:12.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007489)

**[05:00:14.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007490)  
Can you give us a breakdown?

**[05:00:16.000] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007491)  
Yes, sir. SG4, that's

**[05:00:17.820] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007492)  
875, sir.

**[05:00:20.220] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007493)  
And SG8, sir, is

**[05:00:21.920] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007494)  
1,004.

**[05:00:23.920] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007495)  
Yung SG11 namin, sir,

**[05:00:25.740] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007496)  
368. That's

**[05:00:27.460] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007497)  
2,247 screeners,

**[05:00:30.000] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007498)  
sir. Ito, sir, yung nagbabantay talaga natin

**[05:00:31.720] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007499)

**[05:00:33.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007499-split-2)

Yes, sir. SG11, sir,

**[05:00:35.740] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007501)  
368 lang po.

**[05:00:37.200] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007501-split-2)

**[05:00:37.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007502)

**[05:00:39.100] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007502-split-2)

**[05:00:39.920] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007503)  
Nasa airports po lahat ito.

**[05:00:41.300] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007503-split-2)

**[05:00:42.560] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007504)  
SG4, sir, kasama rin po.

**[05:00:46.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007505)  
When did you write the letter

**[05:00:47.880] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007506)

**[05:00:49.100] OTS Administrator Gilbert DC. Cruz** (asr-007507)  
I'll check, sir, and I'll let you.

**[05:00:51.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007508)

**[05:00:53.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007509)

**[05:00:54.340] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007510)

**[05:00:57.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007511)

**[05:00:59.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007512)

Yung subsistence increased

**[05:01:01.580] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007514)

**[05:01:03.680] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007515)

**[05:01:06.260] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007516)  
150 to 350 a day,

**[05:01:08.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007517)  
yung subsistence.

**[05:01:09.760] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007518)  
Siyempre, we all want also to

**[05:01:12.140] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007519)

**[05:01:14.780] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007520)

**[05:01:24.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007525)

**[05:01:26.240] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007526)  
ng

**[05:01:26.520] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007527)

**[05:01:34.640] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007530)

**[05:01:35.940] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007531)

SG, ano to, mga, these are

**[05:01:41.980] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007534)  
So, can you

**[05:01:44.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007535)

**[05:01:46.080] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007536)

Thank you, Sec.

**[05:16:06.040] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007928)  
So target next year, operations?

**[05:16:09.220] CAAP Deputy Director General for Administration Atty. Danjun G. Lucas** (asr-007929)  
Yes, yes, Senator. Mr. Chair.

**[05:16:11.420] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007930)  
Tapos na ba yung runway? Finish na except for the culvert? Yung ginagawa niyong culvert?

**[05:16:16.540] CAAP Deputy Director General for Administration Atty. Danjun G. Lucas** (asr-007931)  
Nagawa na po yung kabuhuhan ng 2,100 pero yun po sa taas ng culvert hindi pa po nakukongkrituhan kasi kailangan po ma-retrofit yung sa culvert sa ilalim. Nagawa na rin po yung culvert but we want to make sure kasi po dinadaanan ng tubig.

**[05:16:31.520] CAAP Deputy Director General for Administration Atty. Danjun G. Lucas** (asr-007932)  
And kapag nagkaroon na po ng operations, magiging mabigat po yung load na para doon sa runway and we're also expecting because magkakaroon ng community around, meron pong mga environmental impact na kailangan naming siguraduhin.

**[05:16:44.240] CAAP Deputy Director General for Administration Atty. Danjun G. Lucas** (asr-007933)  
So we made it.

**[05:16:46.540] CAAP Deputy Director General for Administration Atty. Danjun G. Lucas** (asr-007934)  
Seemed it prudent as approved by the Secretary and the Director General na i-retrofit pa po para sigurado po tayo kasi running water po yung nandun sa ilalim. So we don't want to risk it, Mr. Chair. So salamat po.

**[05:17:01.620] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007935)  
Okay, thank you. Yan yung mga ano eh. Hopefully, pag natapos yung bukid nun, sana kung masimula din na yung mati, that will be good addition especially for Mindanao. Meron isa sa southern part and then eastern side.

**[05:17:16.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007936)

**[05:18:05.200] DOTr Secretary Giovanni Z. Lopez** (asr-007943)

**[05:18:44.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007945)

**[05:19:00.280] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007948)  
With Marina, I'll just like to ask Marina. Miss Sonia Malaluan, ating administrator. Meron ba kayong plano na i-modernize yung sea service record ng Sea Man's Record Book?

**[05:19:15.160] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007949)  
Hindi nyo pa ba?

**[05:19:16.540] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007950)  
Gagawing digitally verifiable, no? Para ang inyong immigration records kaysa physical passport, wet stamp.

**[05:19:24.340] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007951)  
Good afternoon, Mr. Chair, and with the permission of the Secretary. Sa ngayon po, may plano po. Kaya lang wala pa po talagang provision ng budget.

**[05:19:35.100] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007952)  
Because ang pinaprioritize po namin, which we included in our budget proposal, is the tech refresh and the data center as well as the recovery system for the seafarer identity document and seafarer's record book issue 1 system.

**[05:19:55.300] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007953)  
But nakasama po yan sa medium term plan to have an electronic record book.

**[05:20:03.020] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007954)  
Sa ngayon po, verifiable.

**[05:20:05.100] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007955)  
In terms of the validity of the record book as well as yung biometric features na nakarecord sa SID, included doon yung chip to have the biometric, facial biometric features.

**[05:20:24.620] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007956)  
So kasama naman po as long as mag-provide po ng additional budget.

**[05:20:30.260] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007957)  
Although hindi pa po namin naisama sa proposal ng 27.

**[05:20:33.840] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007958)  
Because inuuna nga po namin na ma-refresh software and hardware of the SID-SRB system that started, which started its implementation in 2019.

**[05:20:50.600] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007959)  
So develop po ito in 2018. So almost 8 years po. Walang update. Walang any revision since then up to now.

**[05:21:00.660] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007960)  
Both in software and in hardware po.

**[05:21:03.840] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007961)  
So we expect, actually we're experiencing frequent breakdown of the hardware because of that we need to replace na po the hardware and have an update on the software.

**[05:21:17.280] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007962)  
So next po yung plan to have the electronic seafarer's record book together with the electronic training record book.

**[05:21:27.080] MARINA Administrator Sonia B. Malaluan** (asr-007963)  
So mayroon naman pong plano.

**[05:21:31.120] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007964)  
Okay.

**[05:21:32.360] Senator Joseph Victor "JV" G. Ejercito** (asr-007965)
